# Proposed contracts — design-side extraction report

4 component set(s) extracted from the canvas dump. Every proposal parses against the contract schema. A proposal is a STARTING POINT: unbound values are NAMED below (never silently tokenized), and each note is a review line item.

- this dump's reader could not see: variable names and multi-mode values (dump v1.4/v1.6): NOT captured because the token lacks the file_variables:read scope (Figma 403 on /v1/files/:key/variables/local — a token scope, not a plan limit): every bound fact on this dump degrades to its resolved literal (the collection’s DEFAULT mode — Light/Default — with no mode recorded), no variable NAME survives into the proposal, and the other modes’ values are absent; FIX: regenerate the token with file_variables:read and re-run; absolute placement on non-shape nodes (dump v1.7): not captured on this route — an out-of-flow FRAME/TEXT (e.g. a corner-pinned badge) re-enters the flow and renders in-line; image fills (dump v1.7 imageFill / v1.9 imageHash): not captured on this route — an IMAGE paint (e.g. an avatar photo) is read as no fill and renders as an empty box; fixed sizes on plain rectangles (dump v1.8 fixedSize): not captured on this route — a drawn width/height is lost and the node sizes to content; strokeAlign (dump v1.11): not captured on this route — an OUTSIDE stroke (focus ring) will be read as an inward border; layout wrap + row spacing (dump v1.12 layout.wrap/rowSpacing): not captured on this route — a wrapping row is read as a single non-wrapping line; the full constraints map (dump v1.13): not captured on this route — MIN/MAX/CENTER/STRETCH/SCALE pinning carries only on shape decor, not on other node types — absence of these facts is a READ limit of the import route, not evidence about the design; re-import via the plugin dump (extract/figma/dump.plugin.js, dump v1.13) to capture them
- semantics.element defaulted to "div" — element/role/ARIA are not drawn on the canvas and the name/axis inference table matched nothing; set the real host element
- Icon:root/icon Container: slot "icon Container" SLOT preferredValues is EMPTY ([]) — an UNCONSTRAINED swap by the designer's own declaration (any component may fill it; dump v1.5 propertyDefinitions); no `accepts` proposed and acceptsMode is left open — declare `accepts` in the contract if the code side should constrain it
- Icon:root/icon Container/ArrowArcLeft: host override(s) on nested "ArrowArcLeft" internals — "Vector" fills/inheritFillStyleId = #f1f0ea in 1/1 variant(s) [Icon] — a HOST fact (the icon colour per variant) on a child-owned node: instance internals are elided by rule and the child contract declares no overridable channel for it, so the override is NAMED, not carried (declare `overridable` on the child's root part and the component.overrides machinery can carry it as a minted per-variant ref)
- Icon:root/icon Container: slot "icon Container" design-time content "ArrowArcLeft" proposed as defaultContent [ds.arrow-arc-left] — a STUB rendering the OBSERVED geometry only (dump v1.5 bbox + primary paint; PROVISIONAL — import the real child set to replace it)
- Icon:root/icon Container: drawn FILL-height under a ROW parent with a DEFINITE height (dump v1.31 fillHeight; the parent's other children hug, so the parent's `align: stretch` cannot carry it) — carried as the part literal `height: 100%` (the cross-axis stretch against the parent's definite box, the same carrier crossAxisFillByProp uses for width)
- Icon:root/icon Container: NATIVE Figma slot node "icon Container" (Schema 2025) — proposed as slot part; regeneration reproduces a native slot, never an INSTANCE_SWAP placeholder

- Icon:root: root height is DRAWN FIXED in every variant — the observed dimension (20px, dump v1.5 bbox) is proposed as a minted root token (the drawn value is the only witness; rename against your real tokens)

- this dump's reader could not see: variable names and multi-mode values (dump v1.4/v1.6): NOT captured because the token lacks the file_variables:read scope (Figma 403 on /v1/files/:key/variables/local — a token scope, not a plan limit): every bound fact on this dump degrades to its resolved literal (the collection’s DEFAULT mode — Light/Default — with no mode recorded), no variable NAME survives into the proposal, and the other modes’ values are absent; FIX: regenerate the token with file_variables:read and re-run; absolute placement on non-shape nodes (dump v1.7): not captured on this route — an out-of-flow FRAME/TEXT (e.g. a corner-pinned badge) re-enters the flow and renders in-line; image fills (dump v1.7 imageFill / v1.9 imageHash): not captured on this route — an IMAGE paint (e.g. an avatar photo) is read as no fill and renders as an empty box; fixed sizes on plain rectangles (dump v1.8 fixedSize): not captured on this route — a drawn width/height is lost and the node sizes to content; strokeAlign (dump v1.11): not captured on this route — an OUTSIDE stroke (focus ring) will be read as an inward border; layout wrap + row spacing (dump v1.12 layout.wrap/rowSpacing): not captured on this route — a wrapping row is read as a single non-wrapping line; the full constraints map (dump v1.13): not captured on this route — MIN/MAX/CENTER/STRETCH/SCALE pinning carries only on shape decor, not on other node types — absence of these facts is a READ limit of the import route, not evidence about the design; re-import via the plugin dump (extract/figma/dump.plugin.js, dump v1.13) to capture them
- semantics: element "button" inferred from the set name "Button" — inference is mechanical (name/axis table), review
- state-axis-projected (DECISION designer-state-axis-projected, docs/23 §D.41): variant axis "State" (Default|Hover|Focus|Disabled) is a DESIGNER's drawing of the platform's interaction states — every value is in the closed table (core/interaction-state-axis.ts), the set carries no ds_contracts stamp and its reader could have seen one. It is projected, not refused: Default→rest (the base every state is read against), Hover→hover, Focus→focus-visible, Disabled→the `disabled` boolean prop + the disabled state block. The axis is NOT a prop; the exact projection holds the source matrix to the contract's own variant axes × these 4 values (minus any undrawn cells named on this proposal), exactly. A hand-authored enum prop is the way to keep the axis as API instead
- variant axis "State" (Default|Hover|Focus|Disabled) IS the platform's interaction states, not API — promoted: the axis is NOT a prop; anatomy and base facts come from the 30 default-state variant(s); Hover→hover, Focus→focus-visible propose root state overrides; Disabled→ a `disabled` BOOLEAN prop + disabled state block
- prop `variant`: the set's DECLARED default for "Variant" is "Primary" (propertyDefinitions.defaultValue, dump v1.5), not the first variant in tree order ("Bare") — the proposal's default follows the declared default, and the base plane every per-value carrier deviates from (tokens / tokensByProp / layoutByProp / textByProp) is the Primary plane (Phase 2 exam: Badge Size, Chip Dismissible, Heading Tag/Variant defaulted to the first-drawn value with no receipt)
- Button:root stroke: drawn in 6/30 variant(s) — the ABSENT variants mint #00000000 (their absence is a DRAWN fact: a strokeless node is a zero-width transparent stroke, a node with no solid fill shows what is behind it — the capture receipts every other paint kind by name, so "no fill" is an observation, not a gap), so border-color carries for the whole axis instead of falling back to currentColor or dropping (Phase 2 exam: the Button/Badge background)

- Button:root/Button: typography varies across variants (fontSize 14/12/16, weight Semi Bold) with distinct text styles (typography/body/sm/semibold, typography/body/xs/semibold, typography/body/md/semibold) — font-size minted per variant with per-leaf text-style identity (exact Figma style name/key)
- Button:root/Button: font family "Public Sans" drawn in every variant (dump v1.31) — carried as declared font-family: Public Sans (a canvas-drawable channel; the return leg sets fontName.family — the face must be available to the renderer, else it falls back to Inter — FC-FONT-SUBSTRATE, named limit)
- Button:root/Button: letter-spacing 1px observed in every variant — carried as a pixel literal, not a token identity
- Button:root/Button: the text box sizes itself to its text in every variant (textAutoResize WIDTH_AND_HEIGHT, dump v1.36) — carried as textAutoResize: WIDTH_AND_HEIGHT; a Figma auto-width text box is a whole number of pixels wide (the advance rounded up), so the code emitters round the element's fit-content inline size up to the pixel where calc-size() is supported (a label that does not fit still wraps), and the writer sets the field back on the node

- prop `disabled`: promoted from axis value "State=Disabled" — a BOOLEAN prop (native disabled attribute on interactive elements), bound to design property "Disabled" (the forward generator's spelling; the imported set spelled it as an axis value — rename consequence documented here)
- prop `shape`: two-value axis [Default, Pill] kept as an ENUM (both states render truthfully on both surfaces); a code boolean is a compatible code-side binding — see extract/reconcile.ts bool⇄axis treatment
- prop `text`: the single text prop carries the component's main content — repo contracts bind main content to code prop "children" (ds.button convention); adopt by setting bindings.code.prop to "children" when this is the label (note-only, nothing renamed mechanically)
- description: carried VERBATIM from the set's own Figma description (1061 chars, dump v1.32) — the proposal-provenance sentence rides this note instead of overwriting the designer's words: "PROPOSED contract extracted from the design canvas (extract/figma dump v1) — API, anatomy, and token bindings inverted from the drawn structure. Semantics beyond the name/axis inference table, a11y, events, and slot accepts are not canvas-recoverable; review before adoption."
- documentationLinks: 1 Figma documentation link(s) carried onto the contract (https://altitude.pages.dev/docs/components/button/) — code emitters surface them as JSDoc @see / Storybook docs links

- bindings.figma.statePreviews: true — regenerating the canvas draws the promoted states as a "State" preview axis (values Default|Hover|Focus Visible, the shared spelling rules) — a RENAME relative to the imported axis "State" (Default|Hover|Focus|Disabled); the contract vocabulary carries no custom state-axis spellings, so the original spelling lives in this note and in the anchors' set
- state-axis-write-back-diverges: regenerating the canvas from this contract draws 45 variant(s) where the designer drew 120. The contract vocabulary carries neither the designer's state spelling nor the state cells they left undrawn, so the writer draws ITS matrix (the rest grid + one "State" preview row per carried state per value of "Variant", every other axis pinned to its first option), in a new stamped set (the designer's set is never edited). It does NOT draw 75 cell(s) the designer drew: State=Hover, Variant=Bare, Size=Md, Shape=Pill | State=Focus, Variant=Bare, Size=Md, Shape=Pill | State=Disabled, Variant=Bare, Size=Md, Shape=Pill | State=Hover, Variant=Bare, Size=Sm, Shape=Default | State=Hover, Variant=Bare, Size=Sm, Shape=Pill | State=Focus, Variant=Bare, Size=Sm, Shape=Default | … 69 more. NAMED divergence (docs/23 §D.41), listed on stateAxisProjection.writeBack
- ua-padding: padding-top / padding-bottom = 0px carried as literals — every variant draws 0 there, and the root renders as <button>, which the user agent pads (1px 6px 1px 6px) where a contract is silent

- this dump's reader could not see: variable names and multi-mode values (dump v1.4/v1.6): NOT captured because the token lacks the file_variables:read scope (Figma 403 on /v1/files/:key/variables/local — a token scope, not a plan limit): every bound fact on this dump degrades to its resolved literal (the collection’s DEFAULT mode — Light/Default — with no mode recorded), no variable NAME survives into the proposal, and the other modes’ values are absent; FIX: regenerate the token with file_variables:read and re-run; absolute placement on non-shape nodes (dump v1.7): not captured on this route — an out-of-flow FRAME/TEXT (e.g. a corner-pinned badge) re-enters the flow and renders in-line; image fills (dump v1.7 imageFill / v1.9 imageHash): not captured on this route — an IMAGE paint (e.g. an avatar photo) is read as no fill and renders as an empty box; fixed sizes on plain rectangles (dump v1.8 fixedSize): not captured on this route — a drawn width/height is lost and the node sizes to content; strokeAlign (dump v1.11): not captured on this route — an OUTSIDE stroke (focus ring) will be read as an inward border; layout wrap + row spacing (dump v1.12 layout.wrap/rowSpacing): not captured on this route — a wrapping row is read as a single non-wrapping line; the full constraints map (dump v1.13): not captured on this route — MIN/MAX/CENTER/STRETCH/SCALE pinning carries only on shape decor, not on other node types — absence of these facts is a READ limit of the import route, not evidence about the design; re-import via the plugin dump (extract/figma/dump.plugin.js, dump v1.13) to capture them
- semantics: structural "button" withheld — the set draws interactive content ("Button": <button>); HTML forbids interactive content inside <button>, so the set is proposed as the default container "div" — a div provides no keyboard access, no focus and no :disabled behaviour of its own; review (make the interactive child the control, or stamp the element)
- state-axis-projected (DECISION designer-state-axis-projected, docs/23 §D.41): variant axis "State" (Default|Focus) is a DESIGNER's drawing of the platform's interaction states — every value is in the closed table (core/interaction-state-axis.ts), the set carries no ds_contracts stamp and its reader could have seen one. It is projected, not refused: Default→rest (the base every state is read against), Focus→focus-visible. The axis is NOT a prop; the exact projection holds the source matrix to the contract's own variant axes × these 2 values (minus any undrawn cells named on this proposal), exactly. A hand-authored enum prop is the way to keep the axis as API instead
- variant axis "State" (Default|Focus) IS the platform's interaction states, not API — promoted: the axis is NOT a prop; anatomy and base facts come from the 1 default-state variant(s); Focus→focus-visible propose root state overrides

- Tabs:root/al-c-tabs__header: per-side stroke weights [1, 0, 1, 0] (top, right, bottom, left) drawn in every variant (dump v1.34) — carried as border-top-width/border-right-width/border-bottom-width/border-left-width pixel literals, not token identities; a side drawn at 0 is carried as 0px (a drawn fact; omitting it would let the return leg keep Figma's default weight there)
- Tabs:root/al-c-tabs__header: drawn FILL-width under a COLUMN parent in 1/2 variant occurrence(s) only — a pure function of axis "Variant" with full value coverage, so the cross-axis stretch is carried as width: 100% through literalsByProp on `variant` (stretch); the value(s) that do not fill (default) get NO literal — their size is whatever their own rules say, and an absent key is what keeps the return leg from inventing a FILL there
- Tabs:root/al-c-tabs__header: clipsContent is true in 2/2 variant(s) (dump v1.20) on a set this pipeline did not draw — Figma's own frame default is ALSO true, so an authored clip and an untouched default are byte-identical here; overflow NOT inverted (a blanket carry would mint a fact nobody wrote) — NAMED; declare overflow: hidden on this part if the clip is intended (review)
- Tabs:root/al-c-tabs__header/al-c-tabs__list: drawn FILL-width along a ROW parent's primary axis in 1/2 variant occurrence(s) only — a pure function of axis "Variant" (Stretch). Along the primary axis a FILL is `layout.grow` (`flex: 1 1 auto`), not a `100%` literal (siblings at 100% each claim the whole row; growers share it), and `grow` is a per-part invariant: the per-variant layout vocabulary (layoutByProp / VariantLayoutSchema) has no `grow`, so the per-variant primary-axis FILL has no spelling; NAMED, not carried — nothing grows this part along that axis in any variant (review)
